\"Never ship a behavior without an automated test covering it\" applies to removal. This specification governs the project's own pytest module `tests/test_remove_book.py`, written by the build agent beside the finished code against observed output. It exercises removal only through the public HTTP interface.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-RemoveBook.md.\n\n## Test Module\n\n`tests/test_remove_book.py` uses The remaining books keep their relative order of addition. |\n| Unknown id | `POST /books/{id}/delete` for an id that does not exist answers 404. |\n| Repeat removal | Removing the same id twice answers 303 then 404. |\n\nEach test creates the books it needs through `POST /books`, reads ids back from `GET /books`, and removes the books it created before finishing, so tests do not depend on This is the terminal story: it runs after every implementation and test story, runs the whole suite through the Commander-owned dispatcher `sources/full_test.sh`, and preserves the command, exit code, standard output, and standard error as evidence.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-AddBook.md.\n\n## Runner Behavior\n\n- `bin/test.sh` is written in POSIX `sh`, with no bash-only syntax.\n- It changes to the application root (the parent of `bin/`) so it runs correctly from any working directory.\n- It activates `.venv` when present, then runs `pytest -q` over the entire suite with no filter, skip, or selection.\n- Its exit status is that of `pytest -q`;
